3:T5d1,Looped models should first be classified by when the model is made compatible with recurrence, not by paper name. Training-native methods such as Huginn, Ouro, Parcae, and Hyperloop jointly optimize shared weights and recurrent dynamics from initialization, allowing the fullest joint learning. They require repeated forward computation, BPTD or truncated gradients, and measures to maintain stability across depths. Post-training retrofits such as RRT and LoopUS start from a dense checkpoint and use continued training to make a dense operator loopable. Published experiments report 3B tokens for LoopUS and 15B short-run / 60B main uptraining for RRT. These methods may add LoRA, selective gates, or confidence heads. They preserve much of the pretrained model but still require training. Training-free inference retrofits freeze all weights and add internal computation through damped-Euler or Runge–Kutta sub-stepping over a middle-layer window. They remove training cost, not inference cost: every extra stage adds FLOPs, published gains concentrate on knowledge-heavy multiple-choice tasks, and evidence on reasoning and latency remains weak. This three-way taxonomy is more useful than calling loop depth a third scaling axis. New foundation-model training should be judged by stable dynamics and isoFLOP curves; checkpoint conversion by adaptation tokens and capability retention; and zero-update inference by window selection, numerical integration, and task-level regressions.4:T503,Use an ablation ladder as the merge gate, bulk filtering to narrow the candidate pool, influence/attribution for diagnosis, and causal inference to check confounding and evaluator bias. Ladders fit the merge decision because they use fixed budgets and evaluations and support regression testing. DCLM’s controlled experiments on data recipes show that transfer across scale depends on capability: MMLU rankings are more stable, while HumanEval rankings reverse more readily. Anthropic’s influence results show that top-influence examples change with model scale, so the results should not be reused indefinitely. FineWeb-Edu, DSIR, and RegMix show that proxies and classifiers are useful for throughput, but every proxy needs a declared domain of validity. Causal methods are better suited to checking whether length, duplication, or domain confounds a correlation, and whether an evaluator mistakes length bias for capability gain. They should not be the primary daily merge gate. The minimum requirements for an internal toolchain in 2026 are to reserve 1–5% of frontier compute for ladders, replace a single loss metric with a per-capability matrix such as MMLU/HumanEval/MATH, and specify each proxy’s validity across language, code, length, and time in release criteria.5:T4eb,RLVR/GRPO moves the model onto new reachable trajectories, while OPD compresses local decisions on those trajectories into a smaller, cheaper policy that often produces shorter responses. The two divide the work of search and compression and cannot substitute for each other. RL changes the data distribution through self-sampling, reward filtering, and repeated exploration on hard instances. OPD turns a terminal signal from one rollout into token-level teacher distributions or preference-like dense feedback. The same mechanism also creates risks. If student rollouts leave the teacher support, token-level KL can reinforce errors. If the teacher sees too much privileged reference information, OPD may learn leakage paths. Reward extrapolation intended to exceed the teacher can cause performance on structured outputs to drop abruptly. A more practical approach is staged or interleaved training: use RL to discover verifiable solution families, then use OPD, self-distillation, prefix distillation, or offline log-prob caching to shorten responses and reduce cost and variance. For small models, sparse rewards, multimodal grounding, and agentic debate, OPD is a useful credit-assignment tool; for capability discovery, it does not replace exploration.6:T9db,agent eval 应以可复跑的判分标准指导优化，而不应只追求新的总分。单条 trace 的判分首先要可信。格式、工具参数、数值计算、权限边界和最终状态，只要能用代码判断，就应通过断言或环境 oracle 检查。无法预先写成固定规则的证据选择、推理依据、风险升级和整条决策路径，则交给校准过的 LLM judge，并结构化保存判分理由。OpenAI macro eval cookbook 先将每条 trace 转成结构化摘要，保留 case_type、run_outcome、eval_finding 和 behavior_pattern 候选信号，再从已判分的 trace 集合中发现反复出现的模式。[OpenAIMacroEvals2026] Anthropic 的 agent 设计建议也强调这一约束：只有当开放式步骤、工具反馈和错误恢复无法预先编排时，才值得采用 agent。工具接口与沙盒必须透明，否则评测反映的将是框架的不透明性，而非 agent 能力。[AnthropicAgents2024]

实用的评测体系可分为三层：trace 级 eval 判断“这一条是否可信”；slice 级 eval 按场景、工具、难度、agent 版本、模型版本、prompt 版本和预算切开，回答“哪里变差”；macro eval 在已打标 trace 上做无监督聚类与影响力排序，回答“哪类失败值得先修”。这三层不能倒过来：直接把原始长 trace 扔进聚类，会让工具 payload、重复状态日志和文本长度主导距离；直接让 LLM judge 看所有内容，又会把可验证的格式/计算错误变成昂贵且不稳定的主观判断。更稳的管线是 deterministic checks 优先，LLM judge 只处理语义和决策路径，再把每条 trace 变成可比较的摘要文档。[AgentImprovementLoop2026][AgentTrace2026]

pretrain eval for agent 和 posttrain eval 的差别在于控制变量。pretrain 侧应测底座可被 agent harness 使用的原子能力：指令保真、代码/工具 schema 理解、长上下文证据保持、执行语义、函数调用参数约束和低成本 likelihood/BPB 辅助信号；它不应宣称“会代理完成任务”。posttrain / harness 侧才测闭环：任务分解、工具选择、观察反馈利用、错误恢复、human handoff、成本和安全边界。Karpathy 的 autoresearch 设置给了一个朴素但重要的实验纪律：`prepare.py` 和 `evaluate_bpb` 是不可改的 ground-truth metric，优化只发生在 `train.py`；agent eval 也需要这样的冻结计分面，否则优化目标会在实验中漂移。[KarpathyAutoresearch2025]7:Taee,Agent evaluation should guide optimization through rerunnable scoring criteria rather than focus solely on another aggregate score. Scoring a single trace must first be reliable: format, tool arguments, numeric computation, permissions, and final state should be checked by code or environment oracles whenever possible. Evidence use, reasoning support, risk escalation, and whole-trajectory decision quality should go to calibrated LLM judges when they cannot be assessed by fixed rules. Judge rationales should be saved in a structured format. OpenAI's macro-evals cookbook first converts each trace into a structured summary containing case_type, run_outcome, eval_finding, and candidate behavior signals, then identifies recurring patterns in the already-scored trace collection.[OpenAIMacroEvals2026] Anthropic's guidance makes the same requirement: use agents only when open-ended steps, tool feedback, and recovery cannot be scripted in advance. Tool interfaces and sandboxes must be transparent; otherwise, evaluation reflects framework opacity rather than agent capability.[AnthropicAgents2024]

A practical stack has three layers: trace-level eval asks whether this run is trustworthy; slice-level eval cuts by scenario, tool, difficulty, agent version, model version, prompt version, and budget to ask where behavior changed; macro eval clusters already-labeled traces and ranks patterns by impact to ask what to fix first. Reversing the order is brittle: clustering raw long traces lets tool payloads, repeated status logs, and text length dominate distance; asking an LLM judge to inspect everything turns verifiable format and arithmetic bugs into expensive subjective judgments. The stronger pipeline runs deterministic checks first, reserves LLM judging for semantics and decision paths, and then converts each trace into a comparable summary document.[AgentImprovementLoop2026][AgentTrace2026]

Pretrain eval for agents and posttrain eval differ in controlled variables. Pretraining-side eval should measure atomic capabilities that a harness can exploit: instruction fidelity, code/tool-schema understanding, long-context evidence retention, execution semantics, function-call argument constraints, and low-cost likelihood/BPB auxiliary signals. It should not claim the model can delegate work end to end. Posttrain and harness eval should measure the closed loop: task decomposition, tool choice, use of observations, recovery, human handoff, cost, and safety boundaries. Karpathy's autoresearch setup gives a useful experimental discipline: `prepare.py` and `evaluate_bpb` are the immutable ground-truth metric, while optimization happens only in `train.py`; agent eval needs the same frozen scoring surface, or the target drifts during the experiment.[KarpathyAutoresearch2025]8:T610,The scope of each HP transfer method depends on the variables its rules cover and the recipe changes under which those rules fail. µP turns width-wise transfer into testable invariants of learning dynamics, Complete-P extends those rules to modern components such as QK-Norm, tied embeddings, and residual scaling, and u-µP adds precision and RMS constraints to the transfer object [Yang2022muP][Cerebras2024CompleteP][Blake2024UMUP]. A different line treats HP transfer as statistical fitting: under a fixed SP recipe, stable data distribution, and nearly unchanged scheduler, empirical formulas or joint scaling laws provide starting points for LR, batch size, and token:param, followed by small corrective sweeps [Dey2023CerebrasGPT][Bi2024DeepSeekLLM][Hoffmann2022Chinchilla]. A third line uses cost-aware BO such as CARBS to address residual error, searching wd, β₂, dropout, and other variables not covered by µP rules within limited proxy budgets [Fetterman2023TuneAsYouScale][Lee2020CostAwareBO]. A more reliable engineering decomposition is layered: parameterization defines invariants and is accepted only after coord check / pre-activation RMS overlap; formulas provide closed-form starts for a fixed recipe; local BO patches wd, β₂, and precision-stability blind spots. The common failure is not the absence of theory, but misattributing recipe drift, depth drift, fp8 numeric limits, or AdamW weight decay effects to “non-transferable LR” [Bordelon2023DepthwiseTransfer][Kosson2025WDMoreThanMuP][Wortsman2023ProxyInstability].9:T546,For coding agents on comparable base models, the visible gap mostly comes from the harness. The harness can be decomposed into four layers: the tool protocol determines what the agent can discover and call; the execution sandbox determines the cost of failure, privilege, and recovery; the edit primitive determines whether code changes are auditable; Skills and Constitution move “how to do it” and “what not to do” out of a monolithic system prompt into versioned plug-ins. ReAct gives the alternating reasoning/action trajectory, SWE-agent shows that the agent-computer interface alone can move SWE-bench outcomes, and OpenHands turns typed actions, Docker runtime, and benchmark hooks into a reproducible platform. Aider, Cline, Cursor, Claude Code, and Codex CLI implement these mechanisms in products: git/test recovery, IDE editor-as-runtime, host-shell permissioning, MCP integration, sub-agent dispatch, and context compaction. Descriptions of Claude Code internals should be treated as secondary evidence. shareAI-lab's reverse engineering and Yuyz0112's runtime patching corroborate each other, but neither is official documentation. A useful evaluation grid should not stop at tool-call JSON. It should score nine axes: tool protocol, edit primitive, planning, context, sandbox, sub-agent, stop/recovery, skills, and constitution.a:T48e,A usable form of downstream scaling laws is now fairly clear: compute should not be the only x-axis, and jumps on exact-match should not be read as direct evidence that an ability suddenly appears. A safer workflow decomposes discrete acceptance metrics into continuous surrogates such as answer log-likelihood, token-level Brier, or BPB-of-correct-completion, then aligns different recipes by pretraining loss or a low-dimensional capability index. Thresholds may remain in some tasks, but this approach allows prediction uncertainty to be quantified. Two engineering routes are available. A model ladder trains a small set of internal models to fit task curves under the target recipe. Observational scaling regresses over public models to infer a capability manifold. The former is suited to architecture and mid-train decisions, as well as acceptance testing before post-training; the latter is suited to data-mix triage and competitor mapping. Code execution, multi-step math, and agentic tasks require particular care because their final metrics may contain real thresholds. Continuous surrogates are early signals there, not replacements for acceptance tests.b:T722,长程评估的首要问题是任务是否已适合委托给 AI，单纯延长输入、视频或软件任务不足以回答这个问题。RULER、LongBench、NoCha、BABILong 和 SummHay 测的是预训练侧证据使用、位置鲁棒性、聚合与长文综合;Video-MME、LongVideoBench 与 VBVR 把同一问题推进到跨帧状态、因果与可验证生成轨迹;SWE-bench、WebArena、OSWorld、GAIA 与 tau-bench 则把模型放进工具和环境闭环里 [RULER2024][LongBench2023][NoCha2024][BABILong2024][SummHay2024][VideoMME2024][LongVideoBench2024][VBVR2026][Jimenez2024SWEBench][WebArena2023][OSWorld2024][GAIA2023][TauBench2024]。

这三类评估不能合成一个分数。预训练侧评估测底层能力：证据是否被保留、对证据位置是否稳健、多跳依赖是否能聚合。Agent 评估测系统能力：运行框架（harness）、工具权限、状态恢复、验证判据（oracle）与人机交接（handoff）能否形成闭环。SWE eval 是公开基准里最接近“Delegation-ready 工作”的一支,因为它有真实 repo 和 patch+test oracle;但它仍主要是边界清晰的单 issue,缺少多 PR、部署、回滚、用户反馈、安全边界与长期维护 [Jimenez2024SWEBench][OpenAI2024SWEBenchVerified][Yang2024SWEAgent][OpenHands2024][SWELive2025]。

Video-Reason/VBVR 提供了视频推理训练和评测的技术背景：VBVR 声称覆盖 200 个任务、1,000,000+ 视频片段、rule-based human-aligned scorers 和早期 OOD 泛化迹象 [VideoReasonHF2026][VBVR2026]。这支持“视频推理评估需要可验证过程”的方向,但不能直接推出“长程 AI Delegation 已经解决”:VBVR 的强项是可控视频推理训练和评测,真实开放环境中的目标分解、工具调用、失败恢复和人机交接仍需要 agent harness 支持。c:T81a,Long-horizon evaluation should assess whether work is ready to delegate. Increasing context length, video duration, or software-task length alone does not answer that question. RULER, LongBench, NoCha, BABILong, and SummHay probe pretraining-side evidence use, position robustness, aggregation, and long-document synthesis; Video-MME, LongVideoBench, and VBVR extend this evaluation to cross-frame state, causality, and verifiable generation trajectories; SWE-bench, WebArena, OSWorld, GAIA, and tau-bench place the model inside a tool-and-environment loop [RULER2024][LongBench2023][NoCha2024][BABILong2024][SummHay2024][VideoMME2024][LongVideoBench2024][VBVR2026][Jimenez2024SWEBench][WebArena2023][OSWorld2024][GAIA2023][TauBench2024].

These tracks should not be collapsed into one score. Pretraining evaluation measures underlying capabilities: evidence retention, robustness to evidence position, and aggregation of multi-hop dependencies. Agent evaluation measures whether the harness, tool permissions, state recovery, verification oracle, and human handoff together support a complete task loop. SWE eval is the closest public line to delegated work because it uses real repos and a patch+test oracle, but it still mostly tests well-bounded single issues rather than multi-PR maintenance, deployment, rollback, user feedback, safety boundaries, and long-lived ownership [Jimenez2024SWEBench][OpenAI2024SWEBenchVerified][Yang2024SWEAgent][OpenHands2024][SWELive2025].

Video-Reason/VBVR provides technical background for video-reasoning training and evaluation. VBVR reports 200 tasks, more than one million video clips, rule-based human-aligned scorers, and early OOD generalization signals [VideoReasonHF2026][VBVR2026]. That supports the direction that video reasoning needs verifiable processes, but it does not prove long-horizon AI Delegation is solved: VBVR is strongest as controlled video-reasoning training and evaluation infrastructure, while real delegation still requires goal decomposition, tool use, failure recovery, and handoff in open environments.d:T496,Pre-training data curation is moving from global rule sets to per-example decisions about which operation to apply. Global heuristics, deduplication, language identification, and perplexity filtering remain the first line of defense: they are cheap, reproducible, and auditable, but they mostly handle stable patterns such as length outliers, duplicates, encoding failures, and template noise. Model-based scoring and mixture weighting turn rules into continuous weights and handle domain and difficulty selection better, but they often emit only a scalar score and do not say whether an example should be dropped, truncated, rewritten, or kept. Programming Every Example uses executable programs to act on individual examples: it generates or selects local operations for each sample, then constrains side effects with sandboxes and validators. The outcome depends less on the slogan “LLMs can judge data quality” and more on three engineering boundaries: whether program generation can be amortized inside the pre-training budget, whether program execution is reproducible and auditable, and whether local rewriting damages long-range distribution and factual density.e:Tbb2,Critical batch size 是随训练状态变化的局部判据，用于判断“再扩大 token batch 还值不值”，不能视为固定阈值。McCandlish 将它与 gradient noise scale 联系起来：当 batch 远小于噪声尺度时，增加样本主要降低梯度方差，step 数近似与 batch 成反比；当 batch 远大于噪声尺度时，新增 token 的边际信号迅速减弱，并行带来的实际训练时间收益开始以 token efficiency 为代价 [McCandlish2018EmpiricalLargeBatch][Shallue2018MeasuringBatchSize]。这个尺度随 loss、训练时长、数据混合、optimizer、LR schedule 和参数化变化，不能将一次小规模实验中的 batch 数值作为全程常数 [Zhang2024CriticalBatchPretraining][Merrill2025CriticalBatchRevisited]。

选择 batch 时，需要区分 compute-optimal batch 和 token-optimal batch。Kaplan 与 Chinchilla 约束的是模型规模 N、训练 token D 和 compute 的配比，没有直接给出 optimizer step 数。Batch 决定同样 D 个 token 分成多少次参数更新，从而将 scaling law 中的 token 预算与优化轨迹联系起来 [Kaplan2020ScalingLaws][Hoffmann2022Chinchilla]。如果目标是在固定 FLOPs 或固定 token budget 下达到最低 loss，过大的 batch 会减少更新次数，降低 token 信号的利用效率。如果目标是固定实际训练时间或集群吞吐，更大的 batch 仍可能合理，但必须结合 warmup、LR scaling、gradient accumulation、packing/length 控制和 data order，适应早期 CBS 较低的阶段 [Goyal2017AccurateLargeMinibatch][You2019LargeBatchBERT][Merrill2025CriticalBatchRevisited]。

Linear scaling rule 有适用范围，不能无限外推。Goyal 的 ResNet 和 LARS/LAMB 的 BERT 经验说明，warmup + linear/sqrt scaling 能让部分大 batch 设置进入临界区。Smith & Le 将“降 LR”换成“增 batch”，以此调节 SGD noise；扩大 batch 仍有代价 [Goyal2017AccurateLargeMinibatch][Smith2017DontDecayLR][Smith2018BayesianPerspective][You2017LargeBatchLARS][You2019LargeBatchBERT]。LLM 预训练里 AdamW、token batch、sequence packing、梯度裁剪和 cooldown 共同定标;超过临界区后,正确动作常常不是继续乘 batch,而是重配 LR schedule 或把并行资源转向 model/data parallelism [Loshchilov2019AdamW][Shoeybi2019MegatronLM][Brown2020LanguageModels]。

2024/2025 的研究直接考察了 LLM 预训练中的 CBS。Zhang et al. 在 C4 上训练 85M 到 1.2B autoregressive LM,发现 Chinchilla 联合放大时 CBS 会升,但控制 token 数后它主要随数据量/训练时长而不是模型大小增长 [Zhang2024CriticalBatchPretraining]。Merrill et al. 在 OLMo 1B/7B 上直接测量 CBS，报告 CBS 初始接近 0，早期快速上升后进入平台期。作者还报告，batch-size warmup 使 OLMo 1B 的 gradient steps 减少 43%，且 loss 略好。这些作者报告的结果支持“动态 batch schedule” [Merrill2025CriticalBatchRevisited][Groeneveld2024OLMo]。f:Tc32,Critical batch size is a local criterion for whether a larger token batch remains worthwhile at the current training state; it is not a fixed threshold. McCandlish links it to gradient noise scale. When batch is far below the noise scale, more samples mostly reduce gradient variance, and steps fall nearly inversely with batch. When batch is far above the noise scale, extra tokens add little statistical signal, and parallel speedups come at the cost of token efficiency [McCandlish2018EmpiricalLargeBatch][Shallue2018MeasuringBatchSize]. The scale moves with loss, training duration, data mixture, optimizer, LR schedule, and parameterization, so one proxy run cannot define a constant batch for the whole pretraining job [Zhang2024CriticalBatchPretraining][Merrill2025CriticalBatchRevisited].

The practical split is compute-optimal batch versus token-optimal batch. Kaplan and Chinchilla constrain model size N, training tokens D, and compute, but they do not directly specify optimizer steps. Batch determines how many parameter updates process a given token budget and thus connects that budget to trajectory length [Kaplan2020ScalingLaws][Hoffmann2022Chinchilla]. When minimizing loss under fixed FLOPs or a fixed token budget, an oversized batch reduces updates and uses token signal less efficiently. Under fixed wall-clock time or a cluster-throughput objective, a larger batch can still be reasonable. This requires warmup, LR scaling, gradient accumulation, packing/length control, and data order to accommodate the early low-CBS phase [Goyal2017AccurateLargeMinibatch][You2019LargeBatchBERT][Merrill2025CriticalBatchRevisited].

The linear learning-rate scaling rule has a limited range of applicability. Goyal's ResNet recipe and LARS/LAMB BERT experience show that warmup plus linear/sqrt scaling can bring some large-batch configurations into the useful regime. Smith & Le's “increase batch instead of decaying LR” uses batch to control noise annealing; increasing batch still has costs [Goyal2017AccurateLargeMinibatch][Smith2017DontDecayLR][Smith2018BayesianPerspective][You2017LargeBatchLARS][You2019LargeBatchBERT]. In LLM pretraining, AdamW, token batch, sequence packing, gradient clipping, and cooldown are co-scaled; past the critical region the right move is often to retune the LR schedule or shift parallelism into model/data parallelism rather than multiplying batch again [Loshchilov2019AdamW][Shoeybi2019MegatronLM][Brown2020LanguageModels].

Recent evidence brings the older theory back to LLM pretraining. Zhang et al. train 85M to 1.2B autoregressive LMs on C4 and find CBS rises under Chinchilla-style joint scaling, but after controlling tokens it depends more on data/training duration than on model size [Zhang2024CriticalBatchPretraining]. Merrill et al. directly measure CBS on OLMo 1B/7B and report that it starts near zero, rises early, then plateaus. They also report slightly better loss for OLMo 1B with 43% fewer gradient steps using batch-size warmup. These author-reported results support a dynamic batch schedule [Merrill2025CriticalBatchRevisited][Groeneveld2024OLMo].10:T61b,The central claim of Interaction Models is that real-time collaboration should no longer be simulated outside the model by VAD, UI state machines, and tool schedulers; feedback timing, foreground copresence, and background reasoning should become part of the trainable system [TMLInteraction2026]. A 200ms time-aligned micro-turn trades some of the traditional harness's plug-and-play flexibility for capabilities that are hard to emulate: proactive interjection, simultaneous translation, live commentary, and visual correction. Encoder-free early fusion lets the main model learn the timing relationships among audio, video, and speech output directly, instead of freezing semantics through a Whisper-like encoder, a vision encoder, or a TTS decoder [Bai2024dMel][Touvron2022hMLP][Lipman2022Flow]. The more important implication for agent/harness design is foreground interaction model × async background model, a real-time form of coding-agent sub-agent dispatch. The foreground continuously receives user feedback while the background runs tools, search, and long reasoning. The two share context rather than switch between thinking and instant modes [KAME2025][MoshiRAG2026]. This approach requires tool scheduling, context-resource accounting, and safety refusal to operate continuously rather than at discrete turn boundaries. TM's batch-invariant kernels and SGLang streaming sessions therefore become prerequisites for reproducing 200ms interaction behavior, tracing its causes, and running regression tests [TMLNondeterminism2025][SGLang2026Streaming].11:Tc7b,判断放在最前:从工业界可复现路线看,GRPO 把 PPO/RLHF 里最让人头疼的 critic 开销拿掉,用组内相对排序 / 归一化标量回报做优势信号 — DeepSeekMath 把这条写进开放权重推理链路的先例里,核心机制是「同一提示下多次 rollout 的组统计量替代 value baseline」而不是新的回报模型 [Shao2024DeepSeekMath][Schulman2017PPO]。代价也很直接:没有 critic 之后,方差与长度偏置、退化组(no 全对/全错)、熵塌缩、以及长链思考下的 token-level credit 设计,全都回到损失函数与采样策略上被重新放大。DAPO 的公开系统方案针对四项工程瓶颈：非对称 clip、动态过滤采样、按 token 聚合的归一化损失，以及超长输出的 reward shaping。这些改动构成了基于 GRPO 组采样范式的可复现迭代 [Yu2025DAPO]。GSPO 关注重要性采样的概率对象：将逐 token 比率乘积带来的噪声问题转为序列似然层面的剪裁与优化，使其与「稀疏末置奖励 + 序列决策」的设定一致。论文报告了 MoE RL 训练中的稳定性收益 [Zheng2025GSPO]。MiniMax-M1 技术报告将 CISPO 定义为放弃传统 trust-region 框架、剪裁 importance-sampling 权重并始终对所有 token 反向传播的工程算法 [MiniMax2025M1]。Qi 等的 DPPO 工作则论证 token 级 PPO ratio 在长尾词表上不能准确代理散度，并将 PG-TIS(CISPO) 作为无约束 importance-sampling 基线。两篇工作的论证范围不同：前者描述产品模型的训练方案，后者提出「为何ratio clip会失配」的论证框架 [Qi2026DPPO]。

HF 的综合博文将 GRPO→DAPO→GSPO 整理为便于入门的时间线 [Uhr2025HFBlogGRPO]。它可用于「路线图 / 术语对齐」，但不构成独立实验证据，不能与原论文的数值结论等量齐观。后续引用图（2025–2026）中反复出现三个问题：(i) trust region 约束的是 ratio、序列似然，还是近似的 TV/KL [Qi2026DPPO][Zheng2025GSPO]；(ii) 组内采样与优势归一化如何避免长度偏置和退化组 [Yu2025DAPO][Zhong2026GRPOUStat][Ahmadian2024RLOO]；(iii) clip 机制是否系统性地截断探索性低概率 token 的梯度 [Su2025CEGPPO][Qi2026DPPO]。R1 类长思考 post-train 把这些问题从小规模数学抬到通用推理预算层,报告量与工程约束都跟 InstructGPT 时代的 PPO+RM 不在同一量级 [Guo2025DeepSeekR1][Ouyang2022InstructGPT]。

选择目标函数前，应固定「outcome-only / verifier-hookup」设定。GRPO-family 适合作为默认组基线；若长输出出现熵塌缩，或低概率转折 token 的概率难以提高，应先检查 clip 几何与采样过滤，再考虑增加 critic。若 MoE 训练不稳定或训练与推理的数值结果出现偏差，应在同一评测协议下比较序列级 ratio 重参数化与散度约束方法 [Zheng2025GSPO][Qi2026DPPO]。尚未解决的问题包括过程监督与末置奖励的混合、验证器的分布外泛化，以及 replay 引入的 off-policy 偏差校正。这些选择仍可能使名义上「同类」的 GRPO 变体形成不同的归纳偏置 [Lightman2023Verification][Lambert2024Tulu3]。12:Td4e,In open-weight training recipes with verifier rewards, GRPO removes the PPO/RLHF critic and replaces the value baseline with group statistics over multiple rollouts for the same prompt. DeepSeekMath is the canonical source for this "group-relative" advantage construction, described as a PPO-class variant rather than a new reward model [Shao2024DeepSeekMath][Schulman2017PPO]. Without a critic, variance, length bias, degenerate rollout groups (all-pass / all-fail), entropy collapse, and token-level credit assignment in long chain-of-thought depend more directly on the objective and sampler. DAPO documents a reproducible engineering iteration on top of the GRPO sampling pattern — asymmetric clipping, dynamic sampling / filtering, token-aggregated normalization in the loss, and overlong reward shaping — with an explicit baseline-vs-fixed narrative in the open-system paper [Yu2025DAPO]. GSPO argues for a different theoretical object: importance ratios anchored to sequence likelihood with sequence-level clipping/optimization, to match how importance sampling should behave under sparse terminal rewards, and reports stability gains in MoE RL training [Zheng2025GSPO]. CISPO is defined in the MiniMax-M1 report as an algorithm that drops the classical trust-region framing, clips importance-sampling weights, and always routes gradients through all tokens [MiniMax2025M1]. Qi et al.'s DPPO work argues that token-level PPO ratios poorly approximate divergence under long-tailed vocabularies. It uses PG-TIS (identified as CISPO in their comparison) as an unconstrained IS baseline. The two accounts have different scopes: a product-model training recipe and a critique of trust-region constraints [Qi2026DPPO].

The Hugging Face synthesis post is pedagogically useful for aligning names and timelines [Uhr2025HFBlogGRPO], but it is not independent experimental evidence and should be down-weighted relative to primary papers. Three questions recur in the 2025–2026 citation network: (i) whether trust-region constraints apply to token ratios, sequence likelihood, or approximate TV/KL [Qi2026DPPO][Zheng2025GSPO]; (ii) how group baselines and advantage normalization interact with length bias and degenerate groups [Yu2025DAPO][Zhong2026GRPOUStat][Ahmadian2024RLOO]; (iii) whether clipping suppresses gradients for low-probability "hinge" tokens [Su2025CEGPPO][Qi2026DPPO]. DeepSeek-R1-style long thinking extends these issues from small math settings to general reasoning budgets, with a different scale of training monitoring than early InstructGPT-era PPO+RM [Guo2025DeepSeekR1][Ouyang2022InstructGPT].

Under outcome-only / verifier rewards, a GRPO-family recipe is the default group baseline. If entropy collapses or the probabilities of low-probability pivot tokens fail to increase during long outputs, inspect clipping geometry and sampling filters before adding a critic. If MoE training is unstable or training and inference numerics diverge, compare sequence-level reparameterisations and divergence-regularised updates under a fixed evaluation protocol [Zheng2025GSPO][MiniMax2025M1][Qi2026DPPO]. Open problems include mixing process supervision with terminal rewards, verifier distribution shift, and off-policy replay correction. These choices can still make nominally "similar" GRPO variants induce different generalisation biases [Lightman2023Verification][Lambert2024Tulu3].13:Taf4,Cosine / WSD cooldown 与 PMA / SWA / EMA 这类轨迹平均共享同一种加权平均结构。「pretrain-time merging」可视为 Polyak-Juditsky 平均的工程实现: Izmailov 2018 SWA、Tarvainen 2017 Mean Teacher EMA、Kaddour 2022 LAWA、ByteDance Seed 2025 PMA、Meterez 2026 Anytime Pretraining 等都把 trajectory iterate 按一组权重平均后再评估或续训 [Izmailov2018SWA][Tarvainen2017MeanTeacher][Kaddour2022LAWA][Li2025PMAPretrain][Meterez2026Anytime]。它们与 cosine / WSD cooldown 互为对偶: cooldown 阶段每一步的有效贡献被 η_t 加权,等价于在 trajectory 上跑一次 EMA。Li 2025 PMA 在 411M 到 200B-MoE 的实测显示,在 constant-LR 阶段做 weighted average 可以「预测」cooldown 之后的 loss,这是这组对偶关系最直接的证据 [Li2025PMAPretrain]。Defazio 2024 Schedule-Free 与 Meterez 2026 Anytime 则把这条对偶推进到 horizon-free 设计: constant LR + tail averaging 在 1×–32× Chinchilla 上与 cosine 拟合的 envelope 几乎重合 [Defazio2024ScheduleFree][Meterez2026Anytime]。

Post-training 的 model merging 是另一类问题。Wortsman 2022 Model Soups、Ilharco 2023 Task Arithmetic、Yadav 2023 TIES、Yu 2024 DARE 处理的是跨独立 fine-tuning 路径的参数组合,不是 trajectory 内的加权平均 [Wortsman2022ModelSoups][Ilharco2023TaskArith][Yadav2023TIES][Yu2024DARE]。两类方法在 loss landscape 上作用不同: pretrain merging 沿同一 basin 的 SGD 轨迹做 EMA,post-train merging 跨 basin 做 task vector 加减;DARE / TIES 的稀疏化,就是为缓解跨 basin 时 sign 冲突带来的相互抵消。混淆两类方法容易导致「merging 总能涨点」或「merging 必然丧失多样性」这类过度泛化结论。

对 pretraining 团队,可执行口径是把 merging 当成两条独立信号源来用: (a) constant-LR 阶段的 PMA / EMA 既是 cooldown 之后 loss 的 proxy,也可直接用于 ablation,每改一次配方都不必重跑 cooldown [Li2025PMAPretrain][Sanyal2023EarlyAvg];(b) cooldown 末的 EMA 与 last-iterate 应同时上报,两者差距就是 averaging gain,通常在 0.02-0.08 validation NLL; (c) eval-loss 与下游 few-shot 不应假设同步,Hu 2024 MiniCPM 的「cooldown 解锁下游能力」与 Li 2025 PMA 的「constant 阶段 average 已经能预测 cooldown loss」对应的是不同机制,前者落在下游能力解锁,后者落在 loss 预测,因此实验上应把 LR cooldown 与 trajectory average 分别消融 [Hu2024MiniCPMWSD][Li2025PMAPretrain]; (d) Boyd 的 EWMM 与 Press 的 NumRec 条目给了 weighted average 的统计解释,窗口 / 衰减率用于调节偏差与方差的权衡，可据此解释 SWA / EMA / LAWA 的增益，而非诉诸「玄学加成」 [Boyd2013EWMM][Press1992NumRec][Hansen2015NonParametrics]。14:Tb6f,Cosine / WSD cooldown and PMA / SWA / EMA-style trajectory averaging share a weighted-average structure. Pretrain-time "merging" is an engineering implementation of Polyak-Juditsky averaging: Izmailov 2018 SWA, Tarvainen 2017 Mean Teacher EMA, Kaddour 2022 LAWA, ByteDance Seed 2025 PMA, and Meterez 2026 Anytime Pretraining average trajectory iterates before evaluation or continued training [Izmailov2018SWA][Tarvainen2017MeanTeacher][Kaddour2022LAWA][Li2025PMAPretrain][Meterez2026Anytime]. The part worth re-deriving every time is the duality with cosine / WSD cooldown: the η_t weighting along the cooldown tail is the same weighted average. Li 2025 PMA shows, from 411M dense models to 200B-MoE, that a weighted average during the constant-LR phase predicts post-cooldown loss. This is the most direct experimental evidence of the duality [Li2025PMAPretrain]. Defazio 2024 Schedule-Free and Meterez 2026 Anytime push the duality to horizon-free designs: constant LR with tail averaging tracks the cosine envelope across 1×–32× Chinchilla [Defazio2024ScheduleFree][Meterez2026Anytime].

Post-training model merging is a different object. Wortsman 2022 Model Soups, Ilharco 2023 Task Arithmetic, Yadav 2023 TIES, and Yu 2024 DARE combine parameters across independent fine-tuning paths, not iterates within one trajectory [Wortsman2022ModelSoups][Ilharco2023TaskArith][Yadav2023TIES][Yu2024DARE]. The two act on different objects in the loss landscape: pretrain merging applies EMA along an SGD trajectory within one basin; post-train merging combines task vectors across basins. The sparsification in DARE / TIES exists specifically to mitigate the sign interference that emerges when crossing basins. Conflating the two leads to over-generalisations like "merging always wins" or "merging must destroy diversity."

For a pretraining team the actionable framing is to treat merging as two independent signal channels: (a) constant-LR PMA / EMA is both a proxy for post-cooldown loss and an ablation lever (no need to re-run cooldown for every recipe change) [Li2025PMAPretrain][Sanyal2023EarlyAvg]; (b) at the end of cooldown, report EMA and last-iterate together; their gap is the averaging gain, typically 0.02–0.08 validation NLL; (c) do not assume eval-loss and downstream few-shot move together. Hu 2024 MiniCPM's "cooldown unlocks downstream capability" and Li 2025 PMA's "averaging during constant LR predicts cooldown loss" jointly imply that loss improvement and downstream unlock are separable mechanisms; the experimental design that decouples them is to ablate LR cooldown and trajectory averaging independently [Hu2024MiniCPMWSD][Li2025PMAPretrain]; (d) Boyd's EWMM and Press's Numerical Recipes provide a statistical interpretation of weighted averaging: tune the window / decay rate to control the bias-variance trade-off in SWA / EMA / LAWA [Boyd2013EWMM][Press1992NumRec][Hansen2015NonParametrics].15:T95c,在 ≥32K 时代，单一长上下文主指标已基本失去分辨力。评测需要持续维护测量设计，仅更换静态榜单无法解决问题。Kamradt 2023 把 NIAH 推成事实标准后,2024 起几乎所有 ≥32K 模型在 NIAH 上接近满分,继续把它当主指标等于在一堆满分样本上做排序 [Kamradt2023NIAH]。RULER 按 retrieval / tracking / aggregation 三族受控负载拆任务,能暴露「nominal 128K 在组合任务上有效长度更接近 32K」这类失效模式;但同样的 13 类模板已反复进入公开 SFT 与 distillation 数据,2025 年底在头部模型上已出现 RULER 自身饱和迹象 [Hsieh2024RULER]。Liu 2023 的 lost-in-the-middle 比「均值 recall」更稳,因为它要求按位置分桶报告;当前主流榜单仍把不同位置的 needle recall 平均成一个数字,会丢掉 20-40 pp 诊断信号 [Liu2023LostInMiddle][Wu2024RetrievalHeads]。每一代新评测也都会以可预期的速度饱和。

预训练团队应按四个维度同时报告长上下文表现。第一,长度分层下的 RULER + Loong + needle-threading 三族任务,覆盖 retrieve / aggregate / reason [Hsieh2024RULER][Wang2024Loong][Roberts2024NeedleThreading]。第二,按位置分桶报告 per-needle recall,而不是均值 [Liu2023LostInMiddle]。第三,同步上报解码代价:KV 占用、decode latency、128K 持续吞吐;GQA / MQA / MLA / sliding-window 在 recall 持平时,延迟差异很大 [Ainslie2023GQA][DeepSeek2024MLA]。第四,做 train-eval 污染审计:每 4-6 个月轮换底层模板,对公开 SFT 集做 n-gram 对比 [Yuan2024LVEval][Kuratov2024BABILong]。

long-horizon agent eval 不应并入单一「长上下文能力分」,应和 pretraining-side 长上下文监控并列上报。SWE-bench-Verified、Multi-SWE-bench、SWE-Lancer、METR Long-Horizon Tasks、terminal-bench、RepoBench 测的是 multi-turn 工具调用、repo 导航与计划/重试策略 [Chowdhury2024SWEBenchVerified][Xie2025MultiSWEBench][Anthropic2024SWELancer][METR2024LongHorizon][Liu2024RepoBench]。这类评测对产品落地很重要，但与 RULER/HELMET 衡量的能力不同。合并成单一分数会将重试策略、工具使用和规划等 agent 因素混入预训练侧的上下文容量信号。两条监控线应并列报告：RULER/HELMET 监控预训练侧能力，SWE-bench 类评测监控下游迁移表现，两者不合并。16:Ta27,Single-number long-context scores barely distinguish ≥32K models anymore. Measurement design requires ongoing maintenance; replacing one static benchmark with another does not resolve the problem. After Kamradt 2023 made NIAH the de facto standard, nearly every ≥32K model from 2024 onward saturates NIAH near 100%, so treating its mean recall as the primary metric ranks models that are already tied at the ceiling [Kamradt2023NIAH]. RULER decomposes the task into retrieval, tracking, and aggregation pressures and exposes failure modes such as "nominal 128K performs like ~32K on compositional tasks," but the same 13 templates are now repeatedly visible in public SFT and distillation data, and by late 2025 frontier models showed signs of RULER itself saturating [Hsieh2024RULER]. Liu 2023's lost-in-the-middle is a more stable diagnostic than mean recall when reported by position bin. Leaderboards that average across positions discard 20-40 percentage points of diagnostic signal [Liu2023LostInMiddle][Wu2024RetrievalHeads]. Each new generation of benchmarks then saturates on a predictable horizon.

Pretraining teams should report four dimensions together: (a) length-stratified RULER + Loong + needle-threading covering retrieve / aggregate / reason [Hsieh2024RULER][Wang2024Loong][Roberts2024NeedleThreading]; (b) per-needle recall reported by position bin, not averaged [Liu2023LostInMiddle]; (c) decoding cost, including KV footprint, decode latency, and sustained throughput at 128K, since GQA / MQA / MLA / sliding-window architectures show large latency gaps at equal recall [Ainslie2023GQA][DeepSeek2024MLA]; (d) train-eval contamination audits, with underlying templates rotated every 4-6 months and n-gram overlap against public SFT mixes published [Yuan2024LVEval][Kuratov2024BABILong].

Long-horizon agent evaluations measure multi-turn tool use, repository navigation, planning, and retry policy, which differ from what NIAH/RULER measures. These evaluations include SWE-bench-Verified, Multi-SWE-bench, SWE-Lancer, METR Long-Horizon Tasks, terminal-bench, and RepoBench [Chowdhury2024SWEBenchVerified][Xie2025MultiSWEBench][Anthropic2024SWELancer][METR2024LongHorizon][Liu2024RepoBench]. They matter for product transfer, but combining them with RULER/HELMET into a single "long-context score" mixes retry-policy, tool use, and planning effects into a pretraining-side context-capacity signal. The right factorization is to run them as two parallel monitoring tracks: RULER/HELMET as the pretraining-side capacity probe, SWE-bench-family as the downstream transfer probe.17:T4c4,Outcome reward plus group-based policy optimization provides a simple training loop for verifiable reasoning, but it does not transfer directly to long-horizon agents. Math-style reasoning usually has an automatic verifier, and most errors occur within one chain of thought. Actions in web, search, code-interpreter, and device-control tasks change external state, and later observations change the policy distribution. Sparse rewards therefore pose both a sample-efficiency problem and a problem of assigning credit or blame to individual actions. Training signals can be chosen by decision count and replayability: outcome reward is often enough below 4 intermediate decisions; step, turn, segment, or process rewards are useful around 4–64 decisions; beyond 64 decisions, single-level attribution becomes noisy and requires hierarchy, hindsight, or replayable environments. Evaluation must follow the same split: final accuracy alone cannot separate reasoning failure, invalid tool arguments, poor search strategy, and environment noise. A useful benchmark should log final success, tool-call success, argument validity, KL/entropy traces, and non-RL baselines under matched base model, data, and inference compute.18:Ta55,有限数据是否适合重复使用，取决于重复的是哪类子集。在 web-bulk 上，重复主要是继续将相同信息编码到参数中；Hernandez 2022 与 Muennighoff 2023 看到的 2–4 epoch 后 effective token 折算率下降，仍是最可靠的默认线 [Hernandez2022RepeatedData][Muennighoff2023DataConstrained]。在 reasoning、算术和长尾事实上，相同结论不成立：Charton 2024 的算术实验、Allen-Zhu 2024 的事实写入曲线、Wang 2025 的 implicit-reasoning scaling law 都指向同一件事——有些能力不是靠更多不同样例出现，而是靠同一pattern 被看够次数后才 lock in [Charton2024Emergent][AllenZhu2024Knowledge][Yang2025ImplicitReasoning]。所以“扩 unique data”和“重复有限高质量数据”不是替代关系；前者扩大覆盖面，后者加强尚未充分编码的 key。将两者合并为一个 global epoch 超参，是 4-epoch 神话的问题所在。

三条曲线分别描述不同条件下的重复收益。曲线 0 是 Chinchilla/Kaplan 的 compute-bound 区间：D 充裕时，1 epoch 的 fresh token 是基准，重复通常浪费 [Kaplan2020][Hoffmann2022Chinchilla]。曲线 1 是固定 mixture 的 uniform repeat：干净池前几轮仍有收益，但收益很快从“等效新鲜 token”变成 memorization 与 capacity crowding [Hernandez2022RepeatedData][Xue2023TokenCrisis]。曲线 2 是 targeted repeat：对 math/code/reasoning/长尾事实这类子集，8–32 次曝光仍可能比同 token budget 的更多 fresh examples 更有效 [Charton2024Emergent][Zuo2025MemoGuided]。Cabannes 2024 的 associative-memory 解释联系了这三条曲线：重复加强已有 key 的编码，不创建新 key；编码尚不充分时产生泛化收益，饱和后则因记忆化而挤占容量 [Cabannes2024AssocMem]。

因此，最低成本的验证方案是一个小型 scaling ladder，不能仅按 epoch loss 的最低值选择配置：3 个模型规模 × 3 个 multiplicity × 3 类 subset，在同 token budget 下比较。loss/bpb 只回答语言模型平均拟合得更好没有，不能回答是否学到目标能力、是否进入记忆化相位、是否污染评测。Hong 2024 证明 representation-side memorization 早于 val loss 反弹出现 [Hong2024ROME]；Charton 2024 证明能力浮现时 loss 可以几乎不动 [Charton2024Emergent]。所以 ladder 的验收指标至少要包括 val loss、memorization window、per-skill learning curve、独立 contamination probe 和 capability eval。这样可以确定每类子集各自的重复使用范围（repeat envelope），而非统一采用“4 epoch 上限”。19:Tb70,Finite data can be reused, but only after asking *which subset* is being repeated. On web-bulk, repetition mainly continues encoding the same information in the parameters; the 2–4 epoch decay in effective-token exchange rate from Hernandez 2022 and Muennighoff 2023 remains the best default line [Hernandez2022RepeatedData][Muennighoff2023DataConstrained]. On reasoning, arithmetic, and long-tail facts, that line does not hold: Charton 2024's arithmetic experiments, Allen-Zhu 2024's fact-acquisition curves, and Wang 2025's implicit-reasoning scaling law all say the same thing — some abilities do not appear from more different examples, but from seeing the same pattern enough times to lock it into the weights [Charton2024Emergent][AllenZhu2024Knowledge][Yang2025ImplicitReasoning]. Thus "more unique data" and "reusing limited high-quality data" are not substitutes: one expands coverage, while the other strengthens keys that are not yet adequately encoded. Combining both into a single global epoch hyperparameter is the error behind the 4-epoch myth.

Three curves describe repetition under different conditions. Curve 0 is the Kaplan/Chinchilla compute-bound regime: when D is plentiful, one pass over fresh tokens is the baseline and repetition is usually waste [Kaplan2020][Hoffmann2022Chinchilla]. Curve 1 is uniform repeat over a fixed mixture: the first few passes over a clean pool still help, but the return quickly shifts from fresh-equivalent tokens to memorization and capacity crowding [Hernandez2022RepeatedData][Xue2023TokenCrisis]. Curve 2 is targeted repeat: for math/code/reasoning/long-tail facts, 8–32 exposures can still beat more fresh examples at the same token budget [Charton2024Emergent][Zuo2025MemoGuided]. Cabannes 2024's associative-memory account links the curves: repetition strengthens the encoding of existing keys without creating new ones. When a key is not adequately encoded, repetition yields generalization gains; once it is saturated, memorization crowds out capacity [Cabannes2024AssocMem].

The cheapest validation is therefore a small scaling ladder, with configurations assessed on more than their minimum epoch loss: 3 model sizes × 3 multiplicities × 3 subset classes at matched token budget. Loss/bpb only indicates whether average language-model fit improved; it does not say whether the target capability appeared, whether the run has entered a memorization phase, or whether the eval was contaminated. Hong 2024 shows representation-side memorization appears before val-loss rebound [Hong2024ROME]; Charton 2024 shows capability can emerge with almost no loss movement [Charton2024Emergent]. The ladder therefore needs val loss, memorization window, per-skill learning curves, an independent contamination probe, and capability evals. The output is a repeat envelope, or range of useful repetition, for each subset class rather than a universal 4-epoch ceiling.1a:T658,The DeepSeek lineage is useful because its successive releases document several hard-to-reproduce engineering decisions in open LLM development during 2024–2026. The early dense model established an in-house scaling setup and a 2T-token data recipe. DeepSeekMoE made fine-grained routed experts plus shared experts into a stable default. V2 introduced MLA to cut KV cost for long-context inference. V3 then documented, in one place, the training stack for a 671B MoE model: FP8, DualPipe, aux-loss-free balancing, and MTP. R1 extended GRPO from math to general reasoning, and V3.1 offered thinking and non-thinking as two operating modes within one product. V4 increases model size while addressing the limits of MLA alone at one-million-token scale. Its Pro and Flash variants land at 1.6T / 49B active and 284B / 13B active, while the main changes move to CSA/HCA plus sliding-window hybrid attention, heterogeneous KV-cache layout, mHC, Muon, FP4/FP8 inference, and full-vocabulary OPD. Read separately, these releases look like isolated tricks. Read as one lineage, each version bump is a coupled trade-off across tokenizer granularity, data mixture, attention memory, MoE topology, router balancing, parallel schedule, numerical precision, and post-training recipe. Useful engineering comparisons identify which choices remain stable across 10B, 100B, 600B, and trillion-parameter scales, which pay off only under long context or high communication pressure, and which shift apparently avoided costs into training dynamics, serving kernels, and reproducibility. Ranking releases alone does not answer these questions.1b:T551,A single scalar is insufficient to describe long-context capability. Claimed window size answers how many tokens fit; effective capability answers how far the model can still retrieve, track, and aggregate reliably, and what shape the decay takes. The key change in 2024–2025 is the connection between evaluation and mechanistic research. RULER decomposes single-needle NIAH into retrieval, tracking, and aggregation pressures. LongBench v2, LV-Eval, BABILong, and Loong add realistic tasks, length stratification, and reasoning pressure. Wu et al. then provide causal evidence for retrieval heads, tying “why the curve drops” to “which parts of the attention map are doing the work.” Long-context capability can be analyzed along three axes: pattern capacity, pattern diversity, and decay slope. Capacity governs how many independent paths can be sustained in parallel; diversity governs whether retrieval heads, induction heads, sinks, and mover-like patterns cover enough subskills; decay slope distinguishes uniform decay, U-shape, and cliff failures. On the engineering side, GQA/MQA/MLA and sequence-level sparse attention suggest that most heads are sparse at most positions already; compression and sparsification need not destroy capability, provided they do not damage the small subset of heads and tokens that carry retrieval and anchoring.1c:T726,The first part of the Context Scaling trilogy focuses on engineering: stable operation at long context lengths, data distribution, and packing. `agent-context-scaling-hyperdoc` (cognitive view) and `context-scaling-4d` (taxonomy view) provide complementary perspectives.

The narrow conclusion is this: context scaling is not about increasing the addressable window; it is about making distant evidence repeatedly necessary for loss reduction during training and actually used for composition, synthesis, and generation at evaluation time. Nominal 128K is a systems capability; effective 128K is a learned behavior, and the gap between the two is the data distribution. PE and positional extrapolation enable stable operation: PI, YaRN, LongRoPE, and PoSE can move a model into longer position ranges, but alone they usually establish “can run long.” The main gain comes from the data recipe: long-document upsampling, length curriculum, and domain preservation increase how often modeling faraway tokens is necessary. Packing is the third variable, with a role beyond throughput optimization: related-document concatenation, low-truncation packing, and explicit separators turn cross-span repetition, reference, and alignment into weak supervision. The RYS / Sapir-Whorf series offers a mechanistic interpretation: the model’s middle layers behave more like a shared semantic space, so long context only becomes effective when distant tokens enter that space and participate in later computation. Evaluation must follow: NIAH measures needle finding, while RULER, NoCha, Summary of a Haystack, LongBench, ∞Bench, and BABILong better separate “can find” from “can use.” PE enables stable operation, data is the main variable, and packing determines whether long-range dependencies provide a stable training signal.1d:T63b,Tokenization changes (i) the reachable string distribution implied by the training objective, (ii) how learning signal is allocated across units, and (iii) systems cost via sequence length and KV-cache footprint. It therefore requires mandatory regression tests, like changes to the data mixture or training recipe, rather than treatment as a “preprocessing constant”. Controlled fixed-compute pretraining pins down the magnitude: with the same 2.6B model and budget, swapping only the tokenizer yields 0.6–5.1 pp downstream variance [Ali2024TokenizerChoice]. Industry reporting associates a 128K vocab with ~0.02–0.04 nats lower training loss. The gain is largely attributed to better bytes-per-token/fertility for non-English and code, which linearly affects KV cache and throughput [Dubey2024Llama3]. Regression tests should also cover the risks: local merges for digits/dates create 10–20 pp gaps on 3–5 digit carry-sensitive arithmetic and temporal reasoning [Singh2024TokenizationCounts][Bhatia2025DateFragments]. In addition, 3k–10k+ under-trained tail tokens are common and require scanning followed by repair through short continued pretraining [LandBartolo2024Magikarp]. For cross-tokenizer evaluation, per-token PPL is not comparable because the denominator changes; use BPB, character-string likelihood, or exact byte-level probabilities [Vieira2024Characters][Phan2024ExactByteProbabilities]. Practical default: 64K–128K. Treat 256K+ as experimental and require fixed-compute curves, systems cost measurements, and tail scans, with digit/date tasks as release gates.1e:T7b6,SWE Agents must complete a repo-level development loop: understand issue descriptions, locate relevant code files, generate mergeable diffs, and pass all related tests. The task distribution differs fundamentally from that of traditional single-file code generation. Current mainstream optimization paths fall into four categories: inference-time scaffolding and toolchain design, post-training RL and verifier optimization, large-scale pure code corpus pretraining, and alignment of training data shape with the task distribution during pretraining. Existing public experiments show that inference-time optimization and RL both improve base-model capability. When pretraining covers only file-level code snippets, the model lacks statistical priors for cross-file references, patch insertion, and fail→fix trajectories. This causes the agent loop to degenerate into random trial and error, limiting the SWE-bench pass rate to below 35%. A more practical deployment approach introduces repo-level structure, development process data, and executable feedback during pretraining or mid-training. Combined with a high proportion of code, repo-level packing, FIM, and related techniques, this can substantially reduce post-training optimization costs and raise the SWE-bench pass rate to over 45%. On top of that, SWE-Agent scaling itself is becoming engineered: sample count vs pass@N is near log-linear on SWE-bench Lite (DeepSeek-Coder-V2 lifts from 15.9% to 56% as samples go from 1 to 250 [LargeLanguageMonkeys2024]); under prompt-difficulty-adaptive test-time compute allocation, a smaller model with more sampling can match a 14× larger model [SnellTTC2024][InferenceScalingLaws2024]; cross-family, observational scaling laws can predict SWE performance without training a new model [Ruan2024ObservationalScalingLaws]—turning SWE-Agent performance from a binary “can it work?” into a two-dimensional Pareto over pretrain compute × inference compute × verifier.1f:T632,A rule for evaluating deployable architectures should use three measurable costs rather than novelty: per-token inference cost (KV-cache capacity and bandwidth), training-stability risk (loss-spike/divergence probability and debugging time), and migration/expansion cost (kernel/serving-stack work and ecosystem compatibility). Public evidence suggests dense decoder-only models ≤70B can default to GQA: reducing KV heads from h to around h/8 yields near-linear KV-cache savings, while quality degradation typically accelerates only near the MQA endpoint (KV heads=1) [Ainslie2023GQA][Shazeer2019MQA]. For long context (≥128K), memory and bandwidth saturate before FLOPs; Gemma 3 reports ~4× lower inference memory at 128K with a 5:1 local/global interleaving recipe, turning long-context defaults into a reproducible template [Gemma3Report], and Mistral 7B shows SWA + rolling cache is deployable [Mistral2023]. At ≥200B or MoE scale, MLA uses latent KV to reduce cache to ~1/7 with quality near GQA, at the cost of a latent reconstruction path and serving-stack complexity [DeepSeekV2]. On stability, Wortsman et al. mechanize loss spikes via attention-logit variance and output-norm growth, making QK-Norm a targeted intervention; sandwich norm still lacks broad independent ablations [Wortsman2023Instabilities][Gemma3Report]. Scaling also has alternatives to from-scratch training: SOLAR and LLaMA Pro suggest depth-up-scaling / block expansion can be cheaper given a stable base, but public negative results and boundary conditions remain thin [Kim2023Solar][LLaMAPro2024].20:T52d,A practical engineering approach is to standardize mid-train as a post-backbone distribution-shift stage. Synthetic data primarily shapes the distribution by concentrating learning signal in verifiable, structured sub-distributions that cover target tasks, rather than simply increasing token count. [Llama3Herd2024][Phi3Report2024][WRAP2024] converge on staged recipes: a web-heavy backbone provides broad coverage and tail support, while mid-train shifts the distribution toward code/math/long-context/reasoning. A workable starting budget is 10–30% of total compute for mid-train: <10% often fails to produce a stable shift; >30% should require evidence from general evaluations and tail coverage that emphasis on the target distribution has not reduced general capability.[Phi3Report2024][DeepSeekMath2024] The risk boundary should not be framed as “synthetic good/bad” but as pool policy: MAD shows recursive pure-synthetic replacement loses tail modes first, while [CollapseInevitable2024] shows collapse is not inevitable when real data keeps accumulating. Use an accumulate-not-replace policy, retain real data as a persistent anchor (a pragmatic floor is ~30%), and separately monitor whether ranking or teacher style reduces the representation of tail samples.[MAD2023][CollapseInevitable2024][DataCompLM2024]21:T4c0,Ranking systems solely by a single SWE-bench Verified score is unstable. When gaps are only 0.x points, harness choices and sampling noise can change the ranking. A more auditable protocol is a stage-wise composite scorecard. In pretraining, use code BPB/patch-PPL only as an auxiliary “looks like code” signal, and pair it with execution-semantics (CRUXEval) and repo-level (RepoBench/RepoCoder) metrics to avoid mistaking template recall for cross-file editing and state tracking. In the SFT-ready stage, disclose freshness (training cutoff date and LiveCodeBench window), default to EvalPlus to remove false positives from weak tests, and add BigCodeBench/ODEX to cover multi-library calls and complex constraints. In SFT/RL, use Verified as the outcome reference and assess generalization beyond it through cross-language rank stability on Multi-SWE-Bench and environment-based tasks in SWE-Gym. In deployment, separately report retry@k, test-exec rate, tokens-per-issue, and trajectory quality. Comparability requires disclosure of the harness settings: max_turns, tool set, temperature, parallel sampling, retry policy, and verifier strategy. Without these details, Verified provides only partial evidence.22:T5f9,SSMs/RWKV/linear attention are best used to balance inference throughput and memory use, rather than as drop-in Transformer replacements. The main driver is structural rather than “insufficient training”: fixed-size (or low-rank) state imposes constraints on exact addressing. When workloads require exact-match copying, passkey/needle retrieval, or reversible references in dense entity binding, pure recurrence shows reproducible recall drops [Zoology2023][Jelassi2024RepeatAfterMe][Grazzi2024IsMambaICL][Park2024CanMambaLearnHowToLearn]. Hybrid architectures provide the most stable engineering compromise: a small number of full/local attention layers handle precise addressing/copying, while most layers use Mamba/gated recurrences for routing and compression. This division reduces KV cache and long-context cost while keeping recall at a level suitable for deployment [Lieber2024Jamba][De2024Griffin][Zuo2022SSAugmentedTransformer]. Migration is usually more controllable than training from scratch: distill/retrofit interaction patterns from a pretrained Transformer into a subquadratic backbone, then use limited continued pretraining to close gaps [Bick2024TransformersToSSMs]. The main evidence gaps are (1) reproducible curves mapping attention layer count/placement to passkey/needle exact-retrieval recovery, and (2) practical non-equivalence boundaries between linear attention and SSMs under finite state size, numerical precision, and hardware bandwidth constraints [Dao2024TransformersAreSSMs][Yang2023GLA].23:T5cf,PPL/validation cross-entropy remains the cheapest and most stable signal inside the training loop: with the same tokenizer, objective, and model family, it supports early stopping, anomaly detection, data-mix tuning, and compute-optimal budgeting.[Kaplan2020ScalingLaws][Hoffmann2022Chinchilla] Using PPL to make release decisions fails in three recurring settings. (1) Across tokenizers/languages, raw PPL is not a common unit; segmentation changes the semantics of the number, so BPB or information-normalized metrics plus a language-balanced task panel are the minimum for external comparisons.[Mielke2021TokenizationHistory][BigScience2022BLOOM] (2) Post-training (instruction/RLHF/preference alignment) optimizes objectives not centered on next-token loss, so behavior can move more than average loss.[Bai2022RLHF][Rafailov2023DPO] (3) Compression/pruning can leave PPL nearly unchanged while task scores drop, indicating that average likelihood misses degradation in reasoning paths and robustness.[KhanalCapone2024CompressionTasks] A more robust workflow is two-stage: stage 1 uses PPL/BPB for training control and budgeting; stage 2 uses per-task scaling laws plus standardized multi-panel evaluation to decide whether to continue training, compress, or release a model, while recording extrapolation error and training-regime assumptions (overtraining, token caps).[Isik2024DownstreamScalingLaws][Gadre2024OvertrainingDownstream][Liang2022HELM][Porian2024ResolvingDiscrepancies]24:T628,“Dedup vs repetition” involves three distinct decisions. (1) Scraped web pools contain passive redundancy (mirrors, templates, long repeated substrings, near-duplicate docs) that wastes tokens and increases verbatim memorization and evaluation leakage. Strong exact/near-exact dedup should be the default, with “long repeated-substring share” tracked as a primary KPI [Lee2021Dedup]. (2) In a bleached finite high-quality pool (curated, licensed, normalized), uniform multi-epoch training is close to “fresh-token equivalent” for roughly 2–4 passes, after which marginal returns decay quickly; the sharper failure mode is exposure skew: over-exposing a small hot subset causes extra degradation and leaves mechanistic fingerprints in induction heads [Muennighoff2023DataConstrained][Hernandez2022RepeatedData]. (3) For sensitive/eval/copyrighted text, the goal is not “repeat less” but “default 0–1 exposure + auditable provenance + isolated evaluation pipelines,” because extractable memorization risk grows with repetition, model size, and longer context, and benchmark contamination directly distorts scores [Carlini2022Memorization][Deng2023BenchmarkContamination]. Per-corpus dedup is also insufficient: the same document recurs across public pools (C4, RedPajama, Dolma), so a cross-corpus fingerprint ledger is needed to bound total exposure [Elazar2023WhatsInMyBigData]. Synthetic self-consuming loops are a different failure class (distribution collapse); the “4 epochs” heuristic should not be transferred [Shumailov2023CurseOfRecursion].25:T4b9,Optimizer selection in 2026 is best framed as a 3-axis decision: scale (≤30B vs ≥70B), memory (state/communication budget), and tuning budget (how many sweeps are feasible). Under fixed hyperparameter-search budgets and aligned schedule families, many reported optimizer gaps shrink and rank flips are common; this is why AdamW remains a default in practice: reusable recipes, predictable failure modes, and μP-style learning-rate transfer that turns width scaling from “retune from scratch” into “light calibration.” Muon narrows near-second-order benefits to hidden 2D weights while routing embeddings/norms/heads back to AdamW, isolating risk via hybrid routing. SOAP follows Shampoo’s Kronecker-preconditioning approach and reduces stability problems and HP-tuning demands, bringing it closer to deployment. Public ≥7B head-to-heads and evidence of μP-style LR transfer for second-order methods are still missing. Under VRAM pressure, start with low-state methods that do not change the training loop (e.g., Apollo / Adam-mini-like methods), then consider methods such as GaLore that change gradient representation. Evaluate quality per unit of memory alongside training-loop complexity.26:T595,The bottleneck in long-context scaling has shifted from “can it fit in memory” to “can the model reliably use mid-context and far-context evidence in real tasks.” RoPE extrapolation (PI, YaRN, LongRoPE) provides a reusable parameterization path from 32K→128K→2M+, but it primarily addresses positional geometry and numerical stability and does not automatically translate into task-level gains [PI2023][YaRN2023][LongRoPE2024]. On evaluation, RULER, LV-Eval, LongBench, Lost in the Middle, and Gao et al. repeatedly show that perplexity and NIAH behave like proxies for “can it ingest / can it retrieve a needle,” often missing failures such as weak mid-context evidence use, unstable cross-span reasoning, and repository-scale synthesis breakdowns [RULER2024][LVEval2024][LongBench2023][LostInTheMiddle2023][Gao2024EffectiveLongCtx]. On training, Fu et al. and Xiong et al. shift the key variable from “more tokens” to “long-document ratio and curriculum,” with an operational target of ≥25% long documents; Xu et al. turns 128K→4M into a staged continual-pretrain + long-dependency SFT recipe, emphasizing that each stage must be re-validated against task evaluations [Fu2024DE128K][Xiong2023EffectiveLongContext][Xu2025UltraLong]. The engineering priority is to establish evaluation procedures and an appropriate data distribution first (≤128K), then optimize systems and stability for 1M+ (>1M).27:T654,Usable long-context capability requires more than increasing the window parameter. It requires aligning (1) RoPE phase coverage, especially low-frequency separability at the target length, (2) a training distribution where long-range dependencies appear with the right mix and curriculum, and (3) an evaluation loop that measures recall/aggregation/multi-hop tracing rather than PPL or single needles.[Xu2024RoPEBaseBounds][Dubey2024Llama3][Hsieh2024RULER] For existing models, 32K–128K mainly calls for retrofitting followed by strict acceptance checks: YaRN’s per-dim ramp pushes interpolation mostly into low-frequency dimensions, while attention temperature stabilizes long-range logit entropy; with a few-hundred-step long-sequence fine-tune, effective context often becomes usable.[Peng2023YaRN][Yuan2024LVEval] For new/continual pretraining, setting RoPE base near the target window and running a short-to-long curriculum sets the effective-length ceiling appropriately from the start, reducing irreversible damage from inference-time extrapolation fixes.[Dubey2024Llama3][Fu2024DataEngineering128K] For 512K–2M targets, global scaling/smoothing starts to show per-dim mismatch; LongRoPE-style learned per-dim scale patterns plus a larger fine-tune budget tends to be more reliable.[Ding2024LongRoPE] Bypass routes (SSM/external memory/retrieval/compression) win on complexity and deployability, but recall-heavy and multi-hop parity needs head-to-head evidence against native long-context baselines, not just throughput or a single needle.[Arora2023Zoology][Goldman2024IsItRetrieval][Kuratov2024Needles11M]28:T5a0,MoE development in 2024–2026 focuses on reducing three training failure modes, with less emphasis on “smarter routers”: congestion-driven token drop (throughput jitter and loss spikes), implementation sensitivity and gradient interference from auxiliary load-balancing losses, and coarse experts failing to specialize (representation collapse). The DeepSeek line operationalizes these into a reproducible template: fine-grained 64–128 (move to 256 only when capacity is needed) routed experts + one shared expert; prefer aux-loss-free router-bias EMA for balancing; keep z-loss as a numerical fuse starting around 1e-3; and treat early (~first 2000 steps) dead-expert count, usage CV, and token drop as hard gates.[DeepSeekAI2024V2][DeepSeekAI2024V3][Dai2024DeepSeekMoE][Wang2024AuxFree][Zoph2022STMoE] The most informative objections are ROI- and systems-driven: dense→MoE upcycling scaling laws quantify ceilings and saturation regimes (token-rich checkpoints matter), and systems work shows all-to-all/dispatch details can erase theoretical sparsity gains, turning “FLOPs savings” into “no real savings”.[Liew2025Upcycling][Komatsuzaki2022SparseUpcycling][He2024UpcyclingLLMtoMoE][Tan2024ScatterMoE][Kim2022ElephantsProduction] Matched ablations are still needed in 2026 to measure the quality difference between bias EMA and aux loss and determine whether fine-grained+shared retains a net benefit after systems costs.29:T71a,Data mixture is often encoded as a fixed ratio table, but a more reliable framing is: ratios are control variables in a training process, and must be accounted together with dedup, bucket definitions, search budget, and training phases—otherwise regressions are hard to diagnose and rollbacks are unclear. Deduplication should precede ratio selection: use at least document-level dedup and add semantic dedup/diversification when feasible. Otherwise, redundancy inflates effective-token counts in web domains and biases optimization toward repetition rather than information density. Near-duplicates can also create overlap between training and evaluation data, contaminating evaluation[D42023][SemDeDup2023][DetectPretrainingData2023]. Second, bucketize finely: 2–3 coarse buckets average out signals and make both regression and online reweighting uncontrollable; a practical default is 15–30 buckets, and all shares should be reported in post-dedup effective-count space[OrganizeWeb2025][Paloma2023]. Choose the search method to fit the compute budget. For small and mid-sized teams, heuristic warm starts plus 2–3 ablations, followed by offline calibration with RegMix/mixing laws, are usually the best fit. Complex utility estimators and online methods warrant caution unless the team can afford extra stages and maintain consistent data statistics and version records[RegMix2024][BiMix2024][Held2025UtilityMix][DoReMi2023]. Finally, ratios behave like a curriculum: public recipes show late-stage 3–5× upweighting of code/math/reasoning is closer to tail capability acquisition than a constant mixture[MetaLlama32024]. Quality filtering often captures the largest gains, but “cleaner” is not monotonic and does not replace explicit ratios/curricula for scarce domains[FineWeb2024][DataCompLM2024].2a:T607,By 2026, attention optimization requires decisions at several layers; single-kernel peak throughput is insufficient to guide selection: training-side exact attention largely converges to FA2/FA3-style “scheduling + hardware mapping”, serving bottlenecks often sit in decode parallelism and KV-cache organization, and variant support shifts from “write CUDA” to “write semantics then compile”. The contested edges cluster around three points: (1) FA3 integrates Hopper-specific TMA, warp specialization, and FP8 tensor cores, delivering high throughput with more limited portability and shorter maintenance windows; (2) FlexAttention/Triton lowers the barrier for variants to Python-level authoring, yet can still trail bespoke CUDA on frontier training’s tight throughput margins; (3) “replacing attention” is attractive in asymptotic complexity, but for retrieval/ICL and general LLM workloads the evidence looks like partial replacement or hybridization, not a default path. A pragmatic workflow is to identify the bottleneck layer first: if HBM I/O and kernel time dominate, choose along FA1→FA2→FA3; if decode/KV dominates, prefer an attention engine like FlashInfer; for semantic variants, start with FlexAttention and only write specialized kernels when mask/score semantics change tiling, memory access, or distributed blockwise execution. FP8 is the main case requiring BF16 controls before rollout: rescaling order, accumulation precision, and outlier-token error amplification decide whether it is controllable.2b:T56e,Code fraction is a budget-allocation decision: for generalist pretraining, 15–25% code (often 20%) is a safer default. Below ~15%, reasoning/ICL often appears undertrained; >30% should require explicit natural-language retention gates (NL-retention gates), and >~40% is more likely to cause interference between data types in the mixture. Public continual evidence suggests double-digit code tokens need not harm general ability: Code Llama continues from a Llama 2 base with ~500B code tokens, yielding large coding gains with <1 pp MMLU drop [CodeLlama2023][Llama22023][MMLU2020]. Specialists use 50–90% code for a different objective: DeepSeek-Coder trains from scratch with 87% code / 13% NL and achieves strong coding performance, but has a ~6–9 pp general-benchmark gap. That gap must be accounted for and partially recovered through post-training [DeepSeekCoder2024]. Mechanistically, code’s lower entropy often yields lower gradient noise and lower downstream variance (~30% scale difference reported), stabilizing optimization but displacing token budget [Gadre2023Overtraining][Hoffmann2022Chinchilla]. Improving data organization and training objectives is often cheaper: repo-level packing and FIM/infilling increase value per code token, so these changes typically take priority over extreme code fractions [Shi2023InContextPretraining][Bavarian2022FIM][StarCoder2023].2c:T4ed,Treating kernel constraints as first-class in pretraining algorithms aligns throughput, cost, and stability onto one ledger: bytes/FLOPs/numeric paths. Practically, start with roofline to classify critical kernels as memory/compute/latency bound, then decide whether to change structure (MQA/GQA/MLA/KV compression), dataflow (FlashAttention tiling/async), or numeric contracts (FP8/MXFP8 per-block scaling). This is not “engineering vs algorithm”: FlashAttention’s IO-aware design moves attention’s bottleneck away from HBM materialization toward SMEM tiling, yet fused numerics can leak into training trajectories as loss spikes and optimizer-state drift [Dao2022FlashAttention][Golden2024FAStability]. FP8 can appear stable on short runs, but drift at trillion-token scale requires scaling/accumulation to be specified in a reproducible recipe [Fishman2024FP8Scale][Mishra2025MXFP8Recipes]. Counterexamples also hold: a meaningful slice of long-context gains comes from positional interpolation and training strategy rather than kernel rewrites [Chen2023PI][Peng2023YaRN]. By 2026, a practical minimum team configuration is joint iteration on algorithms, kernels, and profiling; otherwise it is hard to separate model issues from kernel-induced bias.2d:T546,4D parallelism (TP/PP/DP(FSDP)/SP/CP/EP) requires hierarchical placement of communication primitives by call frequency, with CP included in mesh-shape decisions for long contexts. Publicly reproducible evidence suggests throughput ceilings at >10K GPUs are dominated by mesh→topology mapping and scheduling rather than single-kernel wins: MegaScale pins TP=8 to NVLink domains, runs PP over IB, and keeps DP/FSDP outermost across pods, reporting 55.2% MFU for dense 175B with a comparable configuration [Jiang2024MegaScale]. For long context, profile attention time share first: if >30%, enable SP to cut activation/recompute pressure without increasing the communication order [Korthikanti2022SP]; if >50% or L≥32K, CP (Ring/Ulysses/USP family) must enter the mesh, since FlashAttention-2/3 mainly shifts the breakpoint rather than removing the need to distribute KV across devices [Liu2023RingAttn][Jacobs2023Ulysses][Fang2024USP][Dao2023FlashAttention2][Shah2024FlashAttention3]. Two questions remain unresolved: (1) whether auto-parallel can provide matched-scale comparisons at 100B+ and >1K GPUs with comparable MFU and topology [Zheng2022Alpa][Xu2021GSPMD], and (2) what public evidence establishes about the FSDP-only performance ceiling in dense pretraining and when TP/PP/CP becomes necessary [Zhao2023PyTorchFSDP][Wang2026veScaleFSDP].2e:T6d6,The second part of the Context Scaling trilogy adds a cognitive perspective to the engineering perspective of `context-scaling-pretrain`. Together with `context-scaling-4d` (taxonomy), the three articles cover data and engineering, cognition and intent, and dimensions and orchestration.

Treating long context as a PE/packing problem misses a practical failure mode: agents still hallucinate even when their context windows are large enough. Standard NTP teacher-forcing marginalizes out-of-document variables Z into the prior p(y|S); when evidence is missing, the model fills gaps with that prior. Hyper-doc pretraining (HDP) treats context scaling as recovering the Z excluded by document boundaries offline and writing it back into the corpus. The objective extends log p(x_{t+1}|x_{1:t}) to log p(x_{t+1}|x_{1:t}, c_t), where c_t is constructed context such as retrieved evidence, recall, tool feedback, or plans. Existing evidence supports implementation: rewriting and reverse-instruction methods yield downstream gains at modest token cost [ProX2024][RephrasingWeb2024][InstrBacktranslation2023][Magicoder2023]; cross-document link pretraining is feasible [LinkBERT2022]; token-importance and learnability-based curricula beat uniform token weighting in training dynamics [Rho12024][IrreducibleCurriculum2023]; tool/retrieval conditioning reduces factual and action hallucinations [Toolformer2023][RALM2023][Gorilla2023][ToolLLM2023]. The hard part is not “make it longer”, but (i) which seed docs deserve expansion (approximate cognitive gap via I(Y;Z|S)), (ii) how to build context C (retrieval-style vs generative recovery), and (iii) how loss masking separates “evidence/actions” from “plans/solutions” that should be learned.2f:T4f6,The third part of the Context Scaling trilogy takes a taxonomy view, focusing on dimensions and orchestration. It complements `context-scaling-pretrain` (engineering view, pinned), which addresses data and engineering, and `agent-context-scaling-hyperdoc` (cognitive view), which addresses cognition and intent.

The main gap in Hyper-Doc pretraining is a structured description of training-time context Z; longer windows alone do not address it. 4D Context Scaling factors Z into three entity dimensions (Space: dependency/citation/import graphs; Time: version/evolution/FIM; Semantics: metadata/tags/feedback/retrieved neighbors) plus one orchestration meta-dimension (intent→plan→action→obs→correction trajectories with differentiated loss masks). The ERS (Evidence Restitching) × IVI (Intent Inversion) 2×3 recipe matrix classifies existing Hyper-Doc methods within a shared framework. The 16-domain dimension-strength spectrum determines whether to try ERS or IVI first. 4D provides a cross-domain construction specification and comparison dimensions for ablation studies. Performance gains still require same-compute validation in specific domains, such as code completion and scientific-literature QA; they cannot be inferred directly from the taxonomy.30:T456,Looped / recursive transformers are neither a Universal-Transformer-2.0 reboot nor an ALBERT-style parameter-compression trick. The real questions are (i) at fixed parameter count, how shallow is the slope when you spend compute on deeper depth-loops vs the 'add parameters at the same FLOPs' baseline, and (ii) where should the loop actually live — in a weight-shared layer stack (Huginn/MoR/Universal-Transformer lineage) or in continuous latent state (Coconut/CoCoMix lineage)? Geiping et al. [Geiping2025Huginn] push the first line to production-credible scale with 3.5B × r=8 × 800B-token pretrain; Bae et al. [Bae2025MoR] turn loop count into a token-adaptive router decision; Yang et al. [Yang2025LoopedLatentThoughts] plot the loop-count vs accuracy ladder on GSM8K / MATH. On the other side, Hao et al. [Hao2024Coconut] and Tack et al. [Tack2025CoCoMix] argue the loop should not be iterative depth but continuous latent-thought mixing — their comparators are o1/R1-style explicit CoT, not dense scaling. Reading both camps side by side clarifies the boundary between depth-loop and latent-loop.31:T4cf,Three things converged in late-2024 through mid-2025, promoting looping from architectural curio to a coordinate axis readable against depth-scaling and MoE: (a) Huginn is the first 3.5B × 800B-token pretrain at latent-recurrent-depth scale — loop-style recurrence stops being a small-model curiosity [Geiping2025Huginn]. (b) Relaxed Recursive Transformer [Bae2024RRT] provides a distillation path from a pretrained Llama-3 / Gemma-2 checkpoint into a looped form, shifting the debate from 'pretrain from scratch' to 'retrofit an existing checkpoint' — the same migration logic the Transformer→SSM line follows. (c) Yang et al.'s February-2025 'Reasoning with Latent Thoughts' renders the looped-transformer test-time-compute curve as a measurable scaling ladder, putting loops directly adjacent to o1 / R1 CoT in the comparison plot [Yang2025LoopedLatentThoughts]. In parallel, MoR [Bae2025MoR] and BLT [Meta2024BLT] stretch the operational definition of 'loop' itself — loops can live in the Transformer layer stack (MoR) or in a patch/byte latent encoder (BLT). In one year the word 'loop' went from a fuzzy idea to a compound family with published scaling ladders, retrofit recipes, and adaptive-depth implementations.32:T6dd,一条更稳的读法:depth-loop 在 `固定参数量 × 增加 loop count` 这条 ladder 上的收益是真实的,但斜率显著低于 '同 FLOPs 下增加参数量' 的 dense 基线——Huginn 在 3.5B × r=8 时接近 7B dense 但没有打平 [Geiping2025Huginn];MoR 在 matched FLOPs 下比 dense 小 ~20% 参数仍对齐 ppl [Bae2025MoR];这些都是 1.1-1.3× compute 换 1× 参数等效的量级,不是质变.loop 的真正卖点不在 pretrain 替代 dense,而在两个更细的场景:(1) **inference-time 的第二旋钮**:同一份权重, loop count 越多 reasoning 越好, 这条曲线在 GSM8K / MATH / BBH 上单调 [Yang2025LoopedLatentThoughts];把它和 o1/R1 的 'think longer' 放同一个坐标系,loop 是 '在参数里 think longer',CoT 是 '在 context token 里 think longer',互不替代.(2) **retrofit 既有 dense checkpoint**:RRT-LoRA 把 loop 变成 deployment-time knob, 无需重新预训 [Bae2024RRT].latent-CoT 派(Coconut / CoCoMix / BLT)解决的是完全不同的问题——把 reasoning 内化到 representation 而不是 token,对照组是 explicit CoT,不是 dense scaling,把两派混为一谈会让架构选型混乱.开放的硬问题:(a) Huginn 用 randomised r 训练,MoR 用 token-adaptive router,但两者的 scaling ladder 斜率并没有系统性对比;(b) ALBERT 的 downstream 回退(同参数共享 12 次但 GLUE 掉点)与 Huginn / Looped Transformer 的 scaling 成功之间究竟差在哪?是数据规模、LN 位置、位置编码设计,还是 loop 粒度(encoder-shared vs decoder-only layer-shared)?目前没有控制实验能回答.(c) loop 与 SSM / Mamba 的 hybrid 混合是 open question——两者都是 'subquadratic 换 depth 或 state',放一起会不会重复计费还没有 ablation.33:T774,A more defensible reading: the gains depth-loop gives on a fixed-params × loop-count ladder are real but the slope is visibly shallower than the 'add parameters at the same FLOPs' dense baseline. Huginn at 3.5B × r=8 approaches but does not match a 7B dense run [Geiping2025Huginn]; MoR stays within ppl of dense at matched FLOPs with ~20% fewer params [Bae2025MoR]. That is roughly 1.1–1.3× compute for 1× parameter-equivalent — not a regime change.
