The real killer use cases for looping are two narrower regimes: (1) **a second inference-time compute knob** — on the same checkpoint, more loops monotonically buys accuracy on GSM8K / MATH / BBH [Yang2025LoopedLatentThoughts]; put it next to o1 / R1 'think longer' and loops are 'think longer inside the parameters' while CoT is 'think longer inside the context tokens' — they are complements, not substitutes. (2) **retrofitting an existing dense checkpoint** — RRT-LoRA turns loop count into a deployment-time knob with no re-pretrain [Bae2024RRT]. The latent-CoT camp (Coconut / CoCoMix / BLT) solves a different problem — internalising reasoning into representations rather than tokens — and its comparators are explicit CoT, not dense scaling. Conflating the two camps confuses arch selection. Open hard questions: (a) Huginn trains with randomised r and MoR uses a per-token router, but their scaling-ladder slopes are never compared head-to-head under matched compute; (b) ALBERT's downstream regression (same parameter-sharing recipe, 12 × 12 loop, GLUE dropped) vs Huginn/Looped-Transformer's scaling success — is the gap data scale, LN placement, positional encoding, or loop granularity (encoder-shared vs decoder-only layer-shared)? No controlled experiment yet answers this. (c) Loop × SSM / Mamba hybrids are open — both trade subquadratic compute for depth or state; combined, whether they double-charge is unablated.34:T534,Through 2024–2025 long-context models climbed from 128K to 10M tokens, while the evaluation community converged on a sobering finding: claimed window vs measured effective-context length is systematically off by 2–4×. Treating long-context capability as a scalar (how many tokens fit in the window) almost guarantees a misread. The position of this report is to decompose long-context capability into three measurable dimensions. (1) Pattern capacity: how many independent retrieve / track / aggregate paths the attention map can sustain at the same time. (2) Pattern diversity: the density and coverage of specialized heads (retrieval heads, induction heads, attention sinks, mover heads). (3) Decay slope: the shape of perf-vs-length and perf-vs-position curves — uniform, U-shape, or cliff. Around these three axes the report fuses the measurement track (NIAH → RULER → LongBench v2 → Loong) and the mechanistic track (retrieval heads, induction heads, attention sinks, mover heads) into a single narrative: ‘why does perf decay this way’ and ‘what is the structure of the attention map’ are two ports on the same object, and they only stayed parallel because pre-2024 nobody could probe both at once. The Wu et al. retrieval-head causal study welded them together; this report builds outward from that weld.35:T637,Three things landed in 2024–2025 and make this the best window for the synthesis. (a) **Measurement inflection.** RULER (2024) extends the single-needle NIAH baseline into multi-key + variable-tracking + aggregation pressures, decoupling retrieval, tracking, and aggregation in one framework for the first time. LongBench v2 (late 2024) raises multi-task evaluation to ≥10K-token real-world tasks across 64 categories. Loong, LV-Eval, and BABILong pull long-context reasoning out of the retrieval baseline. The net effect: 100+ models can now be compared on the same tasks for the shape of their decay curve. (b) **Mechanistic inflection.** Wu et al. (2024) identify retrieval heads on LLaMA-2 / Mistral / Yi and provide causal evidence via head-mask ablation — masking the top-5% retrieval heads collapses NIAH to a random baseline. StreamingLLM (2023) makes attention sinks a system-level dependency for streaming inference. Anthropic’s induction heads and function vectors pin down the phase-transition mechanism for in-context learning. The three lines converge on a single explanation: attention patterns are sparse and specialized. (c) **Engineering inflection.** MLA / GQA / MQA compress KV cache to 5–10% without breaking retrieval-head behavior, meaning the mechanistic story survives aggressive compression. DeepSeek V3.2-style sequence-level sparse attention (NSA / MoBA family) directly exploits the fact that most heads are sparse at most positions. Reading any one of measurement, mechanism, or engineering in isolation through this period misses how the loop closes.36:T8e6,读完应能回答下面 7 个钉死的问题：(1) **NIAH 还能做 long-context 主指标吗？** 给出 NIAH 在 32K+ 下为何普遍饱和、用 RULER 的哪几个子任务可以把已饱和的模型拉开 30+ 分差；明确 LongBench v2 与 NIAH 在『retrieve』和『aggregate』两轴上的相关性差异。(2) **lost-in-the-middle 是 PE 问题、训练分布问题还是 sink 问题？** 给出 Liu et al. (2023) 的 U-shape 与 Xiao et al. StreamingLLM 的 sink 现象、Press et al. ALiBi、Chen et al. PI 的位置插值之间的 disentangle 实验；指出在哪种情形下 U-shape 主要由训练长度截断引起、哪种情形下由 attention sink 的优先级偏置引起。(3) **retrieval heads 是不是 transferable？** Wu et al. 在 LLaMA-2 / Mistral 验证了，但跨族系（Qwen / Yi / DeepSeek）的 retrieval-head 数量、位置、起点是否一致？不一致时如何判定？这关系到 distill-to-dense / pruning 的安全边界。(4) **attention sink 在 MoE / MLA 下还成立吗？** MLA 把 K/V 共同压到 latent，重建出的 K/V 是否保留 sink 行为？给出在 V2 / V3 上的实测证据或合理推测。(5) **capacity 的可测度量** 怎么定义？per-token entropy、跨 head Jaccard、effective rank、entropy budget 中哪个对 RULER 任务的相关性最强？给出 ranked correlation 表。(6) **diversity 的相变** 与 ICL 突现相变的关系：Olsson et al. (induction heads) 给出 ICL 的相变；retrieval-heads 的相变在哪？两条相变是否在同一 compute 范围出现？(7) **稀疏 attention（NSA / MoBA / DSA / Sparse Attention）会不会重写本报告**：如果大多数 head 在大多数位置上 attention prob ≈ 0，那 capacity 是不是被严重高估？给出在 V3 / V3.2 / NSA 这一年的最新证据下的看法。和 `long-context-rope-ntk`（PE 机制）、`length-scaling-pretraining`（数据 + 系统）、`context-scaling-pretrain`（三轴：数据/packing/induction）、`packing-masking-length`（packing 工程）互补：那四个守『如何把模型训得到/扩展到长上下文』，本篇守『当模型已经名义上拥有长上下文之后，能力到底是什么、怎么测、为什么会衰退』——评估为先、机制为骨、工程为收尾。37:T8e5,The reader should leave with answers to seven pinned questions. (1) **Is NIAH still a viable primary metric for long context?** Show why NIAH saturates above 32K, which RULER subtasks open 30+ point gaps among saturated models, and how LongBench v2 splits the retrieve and aggregate axes differently from NIAH. Complementary to `long-context-rope-ntk` (PE mechanics), `length-scaling-pretraining` (data + systems), `context-scaling-pretrain` (data / packing / induction), and `packing-masking-length` (packing engineering). Those four cover ‘how to train or extend a model to long context’; this one covers ‘once a model nominally has long context, what is its capability, how do we measure it, and why does it decay’ — evaluation-first, mechanism-spine, engineering-tail.38:T4be,Operationalize downstream prediction. The 'emergence is unpredictable' instinct (Wei 2022) has been deconstructed in two moves over 2023–2024. (1) Schaeffer attributes most 'jumps' to discrete-metric non-linearity; replace exact-match with a continuous surrogate (token-level Brier, BPB-of-correct-completion) and the curve usually flattens to monotonic. (2) Du swaps the x-axis from compute to pretraining loss, unifying downstream trajectories across dense/sparse, architectures, and token budgets. The engineering gap is closed by Bhagia's compute-efficient model ladder and Ruan's observational scaling laws: a sub-1B ladder of small models — or a regression on already-public models — can answer 'which of these 5 mixes / 2 architectures / 3 mid-train recipes wins' before the main pretrain runs. The real questions are no longer 'can we predict' but 'how narrow does the prediction band need to be for a ship/no-ship decision', 'does the curve survive post-training transforms (SFT/RLHF/distillation/o1-style RL)', and 'when should we honestly retreat to broken / multi-phase fits (Caballero)'. Downstream scaling laws have moved from theory to a 'budget + acceptance' craft owned by pretraining teams.39:T467,Three evidence streams matured in 2024–2025 and pushed downstream prediction from research talk into production SOP. (a) **Emergence reframed**: Schaeffer 2023 + Du 2024 strip out the discrete-metric jump and the compute-axis illusion, leaving one continuous curve on the pretraining-loss axis. This kills the engineering excuse 'it's emergent so we can't predict'. (b) **Affordable prediction tooling**: Bhagia's model ladder (small-model rungs → per-task power law) plus Ruan's observational scaling (public models → low-d capability manifold) jointly drop the marginal cost of one prediction to sub-1% of the main pretrain budget. (c) **At-scale teams are actually using it**: DataComp-LM, OLMo, MAP-Neo, DeepSeek-LLM/V3 tech reports treat the prediction curve as a decision appendix, not a post-hoc footnote; Pythia/Cerebras-GPT-style scaling suites give observational and ladder methods free training data via the (size × ckpt) grid. Next year's differentiation isn't 'do you have a scaling law' — it's 'how narrow is your prediction band, on which task families does it break, and does it survive post-training'.3a:T82d,读完应回答五个有棱角的问题：(1) **指标造假说**：Schaeffer 把涌现归因到离散指标的非线性。但很多业务里离散指标（exact-match、pass@1）就是验收度量，不能轻易换。在哪些任务族用 BPB-of-correct-completion 作 surrogate 是诚实的，在哪些（如 code execution pass）用替代度量就是自欺？给出具体的 surrogate 选择树。(2) **梯子 vs 观测的算力分水岭**：Bhagia 训若干 sub-1B 模型外推、Ruan 仅靠公开模型回归。在 'data-mix 选择 / arch 选择 / 超参选择' 三类决策上，break-even 算力点各在哪？给出经验法则（如 'arch 决策必须梯子，data 决策可观测'）而不是 'depends'。(3) **分布漂移与 capability manifold**：Ruan 的低维 manifold 默认公开模型空间是平稳的；当 RLHF + reasoning RL（o1 风格）让公开模型的『后训练分量』占比急剧上升，PCA 主成分会变。如何在 manifold 上检测 drift？给出可监控的 invariant（如 cap-axis 与 pretrain-loss-axis 的相关性下界）。(4) **阈值是否本身可预测**：Du 的损失轴消除了 compute-axis 假阶跃，但若任务真有阈值（如多步算术），阈值 $\mathcal{L}^\star$ 是否也能从 capability/loss 曲线外推？还是只能事后用 Caballero 的 broken law 拟合？给出可证伪的预测：『从 ladder 拟出的 $\mathcal{L}^\star$ 与最终主训实测应在 ±X% 以内，否则切回 broken-law fit』。(5) **生产决策树**：100B-token / 7B-model 主训前的 5-mix 中标决策。(a) 用 1B/2B/4B 梯子拟逐任务幂律 + 80% CI；(b) 只看 1B 验证 BPB delta；(c) 直接全跑。给出按任务族（knowledge / 多步推理 / 代码 / 数学 / agentic）的推荐方案以及 ladder 与 BPB 何时给出冲突结论的诊断流程。和 `perplexity-downstream-performance` 明确互补：那篇守『PPL 何时还能信』，本篇守『当 PPL 不够时怎么把曲线工程化』；和 `scaling-laws-llm` 互补：那篇守『loss 轴的幂律』，本篇守『loss → 任务的传递函数』。3b:T8c5,The reader should leave with sharp answers to five questions. (1) **Metric-artifact thesis**: Schaeffer reduces emergence to discrete-metric nonlinearity. But many production metrics (exact-match, pass@1) ARE the acceptance metric. (4) **FP8 per-block boundaries**: which ops stay BF16 because 'numerically must' vs 'we didn't bother'? Which training segments show instability spikes? Are V3's fixes (fine-grained scaling, BF16 accumulation, BF16 fallbacks for selected GEMMs) over-engineering at smaller scale? (5) **GRPO vs PPO real differences**: DeepSeekMath [DeepSeekMath2024] introduces GRPO mainly to drop the critic and replace advantage estimates with a group baseline; R1 [DeepSeekR12025] extends it from math to general reasoning. Give GRPO-vs-PPO bias / variance curves across (sparse/dense reward, group size G, KL coefficient), and mark GRPO's known failure regions (tiny G, sparse reward reduced to format-only). (6) **R1 → V3.1 hybrid thinking is a post-training architectural decision**: how does one base host fast-chat and long-thinking KV regimes simultaneously? Prompt-level switch, LoRA-style sub-model, or fully shared weights with sample-time split? State V3.1 [DeepSeekV31]'s actual setup and the trade-off vs R1's distill-to-dense path. Complementary to `transformer-arch-improvements`, `moe-landscape`, `scaling-laws-llm`, `scaling-laws-downstream-tasks` — those track one engineering sub-axis each; this one is the engineer-facing cross-section through one specific lineage.40:T493,The reader should leave with sharp answers to four questions: (1) IDP learns $q_{idp}(y \mid S) = \int p(y \mid S, z) p(z \mid S) dz$ — that $p(z \mid S)$ IS the inductive bias, which IS hallucination. Can we quantify this causal chain with held-out experiments? (2) HDP builds $C$ two ways: Evidence Restitching (detect cognitive gap → simulate search/recall/hypothesize → retriever → augment, optionally recursive); Intent Inversion (reverse-synthesize prompt/plan from doc, back-translation-style). Which seed doc types route to which method? (3) Loss mask is load-bearing: augmented $C$ not in loss, action tokens not in loss, plan and solution in loss — how does this structure map onto trajectory-replay? (4) Landing order: GitHub commits/PR (densest planning signal), web hyperlink expansion, paper/blog citation expansion, SWE agent trajectory replay — which track has the highest ROI? Give concrete seed → augmentation → loss-mask → eval pipelines, not generic 'synthetic data' hand-waving. Explicitly complementary to `context-scaling-pretrain` — that report owns 'run long, use long'; this one owns 'don't hallucinate, don't overfit prior'.41:T68c,`context-scaling-pretrain` answered 'can we run long' (engineering view). `agent-context-scaling-hyperdoc` answered 'how do we reconstruct out-of-doc $Z$' (methods view, via Evidence Restitching and Intent Inversion). Neither answered a structural question: **what is $Z$ actually made of, and can we write a cross-domain construction checklist?** 4D Context Scaling answers: $Z$ factors into three disjoint *entity dimensions* (Space: dependency / citation / import graphs; Time: version / evolution / FIM; Semantics: meta / tag / feedback / retrieved neighbors) plus one *meta-dimension* (Orchestration: composing the first three into an intent→plan→action→obs→correction cognitive trajectory with explicit loss-mask rules). Three load-bearing claims: (1) the 3+1 asymmetry matters — Space/Time/Semantics are $Z$-sources, Orchestration is a composition rule. Treating them as four equal dimensions confuses readers; (2) ERS × IVI (two construction methods) × Space / Time / Semantics (three dimensions) yields a concrete **2×3 recipe matrix**, with every cell mappable to published prior art (ERS×Space = RepoCoder / Stack v2; ERS×Time = FIM / diff packing; ERS×Semantics = similarity-retrieved injection; IVI×Space = invert missing citations; IVI×Time = invert commit message from diff; IVI×Semantics = ProX / Rephrase-the-Web / Self-Align); (3) the 16-domain table needs a **domain-strength spectrum** overlay (● strong, ◐ partial, ○ implicit) — medical is strong in Time, weak in Space; code is strong everywhere; legal is strong in Space, weak in Time. This spectrum tells practitioners *where* ERS lands easily and *where* only IVI will work.42:T9a8,这是姐妹三部曲的第三篇（taxonomy 视角），一定要跟前两篇显式互相引用，不要写成一篇孤立的 survey：`context-scaling-pretrain` 管工程视角（PE + data + packing），`agent-context-scaling-hyperdoc` 管方法论视角（ERS + IVI），本篇管结构视角（4D 分解）。读完应该能回答六个有棱角的问题：(1) 空间/时间/语义 真的互不重叠吗？FIM 看起来既是时间操作也是空间操作，该怎么归类？答：FIM 是『填缺』这个通用操作的 Z-agnostic 实现，它的 *neighbor relation* 可以是时间的（前后版本）或空间的（同级函数），本篇应在 §3.2 明确这点，不回避。(2) 为什么 Orchestration 是 *元维度* 而不是第 4 个平级维度？因为空间/时间/语义都是 *Z 来自哪里*，Orchestration 是 *$Z$ 怎么排进样本* ——它是组合规则，不是来源。写作上必须呈现这个 3+1 非对称。(3) 2×3 recipe matrix 的 6 个格子，每格至少一篇明确 anchor paper 并给出 worked example；不给就是偷懒。(4) 16 个领域的 domain-strength spectrum（●/◐/○）必须出现——这是本报告最落地的交付物，告诉读者 *在哪个领域做 ERS/IVI 最划算*。(5) loss mask 的规定不能只藏在一处：augmented $C$ 不计 loss、action tokens 不计 loss、plan/final artifact 计 loss——至少在三个位置重申（3.2 节、pipeline 第 5 步、领域落地 worked example 里）。(6) 显式对撞：4D HDP ≠ pure RAG（那是 inference-time only）、≠ packing（那是 1D 空间）、≠ rephrase（那是 1D 语义）、≠ trajectory distillation（那只是 lucky subset of Orchestration）、≠ r1 self-reflection（那是 hindsight-after-the-fact）、≠ plan-before-code (我们是全 4D，他只占 Orchestration 一角）。风格：跟前两篇一致——NeurIPS 立场论文口气，带数学（$Z$、$I(Y;Z|S)$、$q_{idp}$ vs $q_{hdp}$ 记号必须跟 hyperdoc 报告对齐），不要写成 survey style 的『X 做了 A，Y 做了 B』流水账，每个 claim 都要带 citation 和对本框架的定位判断。16 领域表请保留全部 16 行，但 *不要* 为每个领域各开一小节讲半页；用一张 dense comparison table + 4-6 个 worked example（代码、arxiv、PR、医疗、Kaggle、chat 各一个）覆盖全谱。每个 worked example 至少展示 space/time/semantics 三维中的两维增强 + orchestration 的 loss mask。43:T9a9,This is the third report in the sibling trilogy (taxonomy view). (2024) 在 LLaMA-2 / Mistral / Yi 上识别出『retrieval heads』并通过 head-mask ablation 给出因果证据：mask top-5% retrieval heads 直接把 NIAH 打回随机基线；StreamingLLM (2023) 已经把 attention sinks 作为系统级 streaming 的依赖；Anthropic 的 induction heads / function vectors 把 ICL 的相变机制坐实。这三条线在 2024 已经收敛到『attention pattern 的稀疏专家化』这一个核心解释。(c) **工程端拐点**：MLA / GQA / MQA 把 KV 压到 5–10% 的同时不破坏 retrieval head 的工作，意味着大幅压缩之后机制端依然在工作；DeepSeek V3.2 引入的 sequence-level sparse attention（与 NSA / MoBA 风格类似）等于直接利用『大多数 head 在大多数位置上是稀疏的』这一事实。这一年里如果不把测量、机制、工程三端合起来读，长文方向几乎只能踩坑。","en":"$35"},"contrast_axes":["测量框架: NIAH 单针 (饱和) → RULER 多压力 (检索/跟踪/聚合解耦) → LongBench v2 (多任务真实长文) → Loong / LV-Eval / BABILong (长文推理) — 评估的层级化","衰减形态学: uniform decay (capacity 受限) vs U-shape (lost-in-middle 位置偏置) vs cliff (训练长度末端) — 不同失败形状对应不同 root cause","claim vs effective: 标称 128K–1M vs 实测 effective 32–64K — 系统性 2–4× 缺口的来源拆解","retrieval heads: 散布在所有 head 里只有 ~5% (5–20 个) 承担 NIAH 主任务；mask 它们能因果验证 long-context-factuality","induction heads: pattern matching 硬件，与 ICL 相变对齐，但只解释 ‘copy from prior context’，不解释 reasoning 长链","attention sinks: 头几个 token 自动浮现的全局 anchor，StreamingLLM 利用而不是消除它，是 streaming inference 的硬约束","capacity vs diversity: head specialization vs redundancy 在 prune 实验里给出可测度量；多样性进入相变的位置与 ICL 突现对齐","训练分布 vs 位置编码: long-doc 真实占比对 effective context 的解释力 > 任何 PE 改造 (与 length-scaling-pretraining 的结论一致)"],"senior_taste_notes":{"zh":"$36","en":"$37"},"anchor_papers":[{"id":"2404.06654","title":"RULER: What's the Real Context Size of Your Long-Context Language Models?","why":"The standard 2024 measurement framework. Extended NTP $\\log p_\\theta(x_{t+1} \\mid x_{1:t}, c_t)$ lets causal attention squeeze out inductive bias.
